using Avalonia.Controls;
using ReactiveUI;
using System.Reactive;
using TU_Template.Models;

namespace TU_Template.ViewModels;

public class MainViewModel : ReactiveObject
{
    public TemplateData TemplateData { get; set; }
    private string _activeMenu = "Overview";
    public string ActiveMenu
    {
        get => _activeMenu;
        set => this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _activeMenu, value);
    }

    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowFileSettingsCommand { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowElementsCommand { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowGroupsCommand { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationG { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationA { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationB { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationV { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowVariablesCommand { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> BackCommand { get; }

    private readonly FileSettingsViewModel _fileSettingsViewModel;

    /// <summary>
    /// Настройки проекта — выставлены наружу, чтобы пометку о несохранённых изменениях
    /// было видно на любой вкладке, а не только на вкладке проекта.
    /// </summary>
    public FileSettingsViewModel FileSettings => _fileSettingsViewModel;
    private readonly ElementsViewModel _elementsViewModel;
    private readonly GroupsViewModel _groupsViewModel;
    private readonly ApplicationGViewModel _applicationGViewModel;
    private readonly ApplicationAViewModel _applicationAViewModel;
    private readonly ApplicationBViewModel _applicationBViewModel;
    private readonly ApplicationVViewModel _applicationVViewModel;
    private readonly VariablesViewModel _variablesViewModel;

    private ViewModelBase _currentView;
    public ViewModelBase CurrentView
    {
        get => _currentView;
        set => this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _currentView, value);
    }

    public MainViewModel(Window parentWindow)
    {
        TemplateData = new TemplateData();

        // Создаём один экземпляр каждого под-ViewModel, чтобы состояние (включая пути и имя проекта)
        // сохранялось при переключении вкладок.
        _fileSettingsViewModel = new FileSettingsViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _elementsViewModel = new ElementsViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _groupsViewModel = new GroupsViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _applicationGViewModel = new ApplicationGViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _applicationAViewModel = new ApplicationAViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _applicationBViewModel = new ApplicationBViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _applicationVViewModel = new ApplicationVViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);
        _variablesViewModel = new VariablesViewModel(parentWindow, TemplateData);

        ShowFileSettingsCommand = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _fileSettingsViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "FileSettings";
        });
        ShowElementsCommand = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _elementsViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "Elements";
        });
        ShowGroupsCommand = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _groupsViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "Groups";
        });
        ShowApplicationG = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _applicationGViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "ApplicationG";
        });
        ShowApplicationA = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _applicationAViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "ApplicationA";
        });
        ShowApplicationB = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _applicationBViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "ApplicationB";
        });
        ShowApplicationV = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            // Do not recreate VM on tab switch; keep user selections.
            CurrentView = _applicationVViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "ApplicationV";
        });
        ShowVariablesCommand = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = _variablesViewModel;
            ActiveMenu = "Variables";
        });

        BackCommand = ReactiveCommand.Create(() =>
        {
            CurrentView = new TemplatesOverviewViewModel(this);
            ActiveMenu = "Overview";
        });

        CurrentView = new TemplatesOverviewViewModel(this);

        // Все разделы созданы и успели досоздать свои значения по умолчанию —
        // только теперь считаем проект "чистым".
        _fileSettingsViewModel.ResetChangeState();
    }
}

namespace TU_Template.ViewModels;

public class TemplatesOverviewViewModel : ViewModelBase

    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationB { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationV { get; }
    public ReactiveCommand<Unit, Unit> ShowApplicationG { get; }

    public TemplatesOverviewViewModel(MainViewModel mainViewModel)
    {
        // Делегируем команды у главной VM, чтобы не дублировать логику навигации.
        ShowFileSettingsCommand = mainViewModel.ShowFileSettingsCommand;
        ShowVariablesCommand = mainViewModel.ShowVariablesCommand;
        ShowElementsCommand = mainViewModel.ShowElementsCommand;
        ShowGroupsCommand = mainViewModel.ShowGroupsCommand;
        ShowApplicationA = mainViewModel.ShowApplicationA;

        ShowApplicationV = mainViewModel.ShowApplicationV;
        ShowApplicationG = mainViewModel.ShowApplicationG;
    }
}

using ReactiveUI;
using TU_Template.Models;

namespace TU_Template.ViewModels;

public class TermDefinitionViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    private readonly TermDefinition _model;
    private readonly ApplicationAViewModel _parentViewModel;

    /// <summary>Модель, которую обёртывает этот элемент. Нужна для удаления по ссылке.</summary>
    public TermDefinition Model => _model;

    private string _term;
    public string Term
    {
        get => _term;
        set
        {
            this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _term, value);
            _model.Term = value;
        }
    }

    private string _symbol;
    public string Symbol
    {
        get => _symbol;
        set
        {
            this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _symbol, value);
            _model.Symbol = value;
        }
    }

    private string _definition;
    public string Definition
    {
        get => _definition;
        set
        {
            this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _definition, value);
            _model.Definition = value;
        }
    }

    private string _category;
    public string Category
    {
        get => _category;
        set
        {
            this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _category, value);
            _model.Category = value;
        }
    }

    private bool _isCustom;
    public bool IsCustom
    {
        get => _isCustom;
        set => this.RaiseAndSetIfChanged(ref _isCustom, value);
    }

    public TermDefinitionViewModel(TermDefinition model, ApplicationAViewModel parentViewModel)
    {
        _model = model;
        _parentViewModel = parentViewModel;
        
        _term = model.Term;
        _symbol = model.Symbol;
        _definition = model.Definition;
        _category = model.Category;
        _isCustom = model.IsCustom;
    }
}

using Avalonia.Controls;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using TU_Template.Models;

namespace TU_Template.ViewModels;

public partial class VariablesViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    private readonly Window _parentWindow;

    public VariablesViewModel(Window parentWindow, TemplateData templateData)
    {
        _parentWindow = parentWindow;
        TemplateData = templateData;

        // Обеспечим наличие заранее определённых переменных
        TemplateData.EnsureVariables(PredefinedVariables.Names);
    }

    public TemplateData TemplateData { get; }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Add()
    {
        TemplateData.Variables.Add(new Variable("", ""));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Remove(Variable v)
    {
        TemplateData.Variables.Remove(v);
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: \template_kernel\src\DocumentTemplates\TU_Template\Views\AddDeviceDialog.axaml.cs
==========================================
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
namespace TU_Template.Views;

public partial class AddDeviceDialog : Window
{
    public string? SelectedDeviceName { get; private set; }
    public string? SelectedDeviceType { get; private set; }

    // Ленивая загрузка: по выбранному имени прибора получаем типы/модели
    private Func<string, Task<ObservableCollection<string>>>? _loadTypesForDeviceNameAsync;

    // Для редактирования: пока грузятся типы для выбранного имени, запоминаем нужный тип
    private string? _pendingSelectedDeviceType;

    // Защита от гонок: если пользователь быстро меняет имя, результаты старых запросов игнорируем
    private int _typesLoadRequestId;

    public AddDeviceDialog()
    {
        InitializeComponent();  
    }
        
    public void SetDeviceNames(IEnumerable<string> names)
    {
        DeviceNameComboBox.ItemsSource = names;
    }

    // Устанавливаем обработчик, который по имени прибора подгружает список типов/моделей
    public void SetDeviceTypesLoaderAsync(Func<string, Task<ObservableCollection<string>>> loader)
    {
        _loadTypesForDeviceNameAsync = loader;
    }

    // Предзаполнение данных для редактирования
    public void SetSelectedValues(string? deviceName, string? deviceType)
    {
        _pendingSelectedDeviceType = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(deviceType) ? null : deviceType.Trim();

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(deviceName))
        {
            DeviceNameComboBox.SelectedItem = deviceName;
            _ = UpdateTypesForSelectedNameAsync(); // подхватит _pendingSelectedDeviceType
        }
    }

    private void OnAddClick(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        SelectedDeviceName = DeviceNameComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        SelectedDeviceType = DeviceTypeComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        Close(true);
    }

    private void OnCancelClick(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        Close(false);
    }

    private void DeviceNameComboBox_OnSelectionChanged(object? sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        _ = UpdateTypesForSelectedNameAsync();
    }

    private async Task UpdateTypesForSelectedNameAsync()
    {
        var requestId = ++_typesLoadRequestId;
        var name = DeviceNameComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) || _loadTypesForDeviceNameAsync == null)
        {
            DeviceTypeComboBox.ItemsSource = null;
            DeviceTypeComboBox.SelectedIndex = -1;
            _pendingSelectedDeviceType = null;
            return;
        }

        ObservableCollection<string> types;
        try
        {
            types = await _loadTypesForDeviceNameAsync(name);
        }
        catch
        {
            types = new ObservableCollection<string>();
        }

        // Игнорируем устаревший результат
        if (requestId != _typesLoadRequestId)
            return;

        DeviceTypeComboBox.ItemsSource = types;

        if (types.Count == 0)
        {
            DeviceTypeComboBox.SelectedIndex = -1;
            _pendingSelectedDeviceType = null;
            return;
        }

        // Если редактируем и есть ожидаемый тип — выбираем его
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_pendingSelectedDeviceType))
        {
            var pending = _pendingSelectedDeviceType!;
            // точное совпадение или case-insensitive
            var match = types.FirstOrDefault(t => string.Equals(t, pending, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                         ?? (types.Contains(pending) ? pending : null);

            DeviceTypeComboBox.SelectedItem = match ?? types[0];
            _pendingSelectedDeviceType = null;
            return;
        }

        DeviceTypeComboBox.SelectedIndex = 0;
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: \template_kernel\src\DocumentTemplates\TU_Template\Views\AddGchDialog.axaml.cs
==========================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;

namespace TU_Template.Views;

public partial class AddGchDialog : Window
{
    public string? SelectedCaseName { get; private set; }
    public string? SelectedUnifiedGch { get; private set; }
    public string? SelectedDescriptionCode { get; private set; }
    public string? SelectedDescriptionName { get; private set; }

    private readonly Dictionary<string, GchEntry> _entriesByCase = new();

    public AddGchDialog()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    public void SetEntries(IEnumerable<GchEntry> entries)
    {
        _entriesByCase.Clear();
        foreach (var e in entries)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.CaseName))
                continue;

            _entriesByCase[e.CaseName] = e;
        }

        CaseComboBox.ItemsSource = _entriesByCase.Keys.OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
    }

    private void OnCaseSelectionChanged(object? sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (CaseComboBox.SelectedItem is string caseName &&
            _entriesByCase.TryGetValue(caseName, out var entry))
        {
            var gchList = new ObservableCollection<string>();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.UnifiedGch))
            {
                gchList.Add(entry.UnifiedGch);
            }

            GchComboBox.ItemsSource = gchList;
            GchComboBox.SelectedIndex = gchList.Count > 0 ? 0 : -1;

            var descCodes = new ObservableCollection<string>();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.DescriptionCode))
            {
                descCodes.Add(entry.DescriptionCode);
            }

            DescCodeComboBox.ItemsSource = descCodes;
            DescCodeComboBox.SelectedIndex = descCodes.Count > 0 ? 0 : -1;

            var descNames = new ObservableCollection<string>();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entry.DescriptionName))
            {
                descNames.Add(entry.DescriptionName);
            }

            DescNameComboBox.ItemsSource = descNames;
            DescNameComboBox.SelectedIndex = descNames.Count > 0 ? 0 : -1;
        }
    }

    private void OnAddClick(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        SelectedCaseName = CaseComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        SelectedUnifiedGch = GchComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        SelectedDescriptionCode = DescCodeComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        SelectedDescriptionName = DescNameComboBox.SelectedItem?.ToString();
        Close(true);
    }

    private void OnCancelClick(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        Close(false);
    }

    public sealed class GchEntry
    {
        public string CaseName { get; set; } = "";
        public string UnifiedGch { get; set; } = "";
        public string DescriptionCode { get; set; } = "";
        public string DescriptionName { get; set; } = "";
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: \template_kernel\src\DocumentTemplates\TU_Template\Views\ApplicationGView.axaml.cs
==========================================
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Interactivity;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using TU_Template.ViewModels;

namespace TU_Template.Views;

public partial class ApplicationGView : UserControl
{
    public ApplicationGView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    private async void OnTypeTextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is TextBox textBox && textBox.DataContext is DeviceTypeViewModel vm)
        {
            await Task.Delay(300); // debounce
            var availableDevices = (DataContext as ApplicationGViewModel)?.AvailableDevices ?? System.Linq.Enumerable.Empty<string>();
            vm.UpdateSuggestions(availableDevices);
            // Если поле пустое — раскрыть список подсказок
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(textBox.Text))
            {
                vm.IsSuggestionsVisible = vm.Suggestions.Count > 0;
                // не теряем фокус с текстбокса, чтобы список не закрывался
                textBox.CaretIndex = textBox.Text?.Length ?? 0;
            }
        }
    }

    private void OnSuggestionSelected(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is ListBox listBox && listBox.DataContext is DeviceTypeViewModel vm)
        {
            if (listBox.SelectedItem is string selectedValue)
            {
                // Устанавливаем выбранное значение только через VM, чтобы сохранить в TemplateData
                vm.Name = selectedValue;
                vm.IsReadOnly = true;
                vm.IsCustom = false;
                // Список спрячем, элементы не очищаем прямо в событии, чтобы не ломать SelectionModel
            }

            // Закрываем popup
            vm.IsSuggestionsVisible = false;

            // Убираем фокус с TextBox, чтобы не добавлялись лишние символы
            var focusableElement = this.FindControl<Button>("AddTypeButton"); // или любой другой элемент
            focusableElement?.Focus();

            // Сбросить выбор после завершения события, чтобы не было повторного триггера
            Avalonia.Threading.Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
            {
                listBox.SelectedIndex = -1;
            });
        }
    }
}

             xmlns:viewmodels="clr-namespace:TU_Template.ViewModels"
             xmlns:views="clr-namespace:TU_Template.Views"
             xmlns:converters="clr-namespace:TU_Template.Converters"

             x:Class="TU_Template.Views.MainView"
             x:DataType="viewmodels:MainViewModel"
             d:DesignWidth="1000"
             d:DesignHeight="600">

    <UserControl.DataTemplates>
        <DataTemplate DataType="{x:Type viewmodels:TemplatesOverviewViewModel}">

             Classes="AdminContent">

                <Grid IsVisible="{Binding Path=TemplateData.Variables.Count}"
                      ColumnDefinitions="* 2* 100"
                      Margin="0 0 0 0">
                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="0" TextAlignment="Center" Text="Имя переменной" />
                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="1" TextAlignment="Center" Text="Значение" />
                </Grid>

                <ItemsControl ItemsSource="{Binding Path=TemplateData.Variables}" Margin="0 4 0 0">
                    <ItemsControl.ItemTemplate>
                        <DataTemplate>
                            <Grid ColumnDefinitions="* 2* 100" Margin="0 0 0 4">
                                <TextBox Grid.Column="0"
                                         Text="{Binding Path=Name}"
                                         VerticalAlignment="Center"
                                         Margin="0 0 4 0"
                                         Watermark="имя (без ${})" />
                                <TextBox Grid.Column="1"
                                         Text="{Binding Path=Value}"
                                         VerticalAlignment="Center"
                                         Margin="0 0 4 0"
                                         Watermark="значение" />
                                <Button Grid.Column="2"
                                        Padding="16 0"
                                        Command="{Binding DataContext.RemoveCommand, ElementName=Root}"
                                        CommandParameter="{Binding}">
                                    Удалить переменную
                                </Button>
                            </Grid>
                        </DataTemplate>
                    </ItemsControl.ItemTemplate>
                </ItemsControl>
            </StackPanel>
        </ScrollViewer>

        <Button Grid.Row="1"
                Margin="2 8 2 0"

                Padding="0 8"

ФАЙЛ: \template_kernel\src\DocumentTemplates\TU_Template\TU_DocumentTemplate.cs
==========================================
using Avalonia.Controls;
using DocumentTemplates.CoreLibrary;
using TU_Template.ViewModels;
using TU_Template.Views;

namespace TU_Template
{
    public class TU_DocumentTemplate : IDocumentTemplate
    {
        public ITemplateMetadata Metadata { get; } = new TemplateMetadata
        {
            Name = "ТУ",
            Description = "Генерирует ТУ документ на основе .docx шаблона.",
            Author = "Маслаков АРтем",
            Version = "1.0.0"
        };

        public Control GetEditorControl(Window parentWindow)
        {
            var viewModel = new MainViewModel(parentWindow);
            return new MainView { DataContext = viewModel }; 
        }
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: \template_kernel\src\DocumentTemplates\TU_Template\TU_Template.csproj
==========================================
<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk">

  <PropertyGroup>
    <TargetFramework>net10.0</TargetFramework>
    <ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>
    <Nullable>enable</Nullable>
    <!-- Ensure NuGet dependencies are copied to bin for class library -->
    <CopyLocalLockFileAssemblies>true</CopyLocalLockFileAssemblies>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <PackageReference Include="DocX" Version="3.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="System.IO.Packaging" Version="8.0.0" />
    <ProjectReference Include="..\DocumentTemplates.CoreLibrary\DocumentTemplates.CoreLibrary.csproj">
      <Private>false</Private>
    </ProjectReference>
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Update="Views\FileSettingsView.axaml.cs">
      <DependentUpon>%(Filename)</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Update="Views\MainView.axaml.cs">
      <DependentUpon>%(Filename)</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Update="Views\AddDeviceDialog.axaml.cs">
      <DependentUpon>AddDeviceDialog.axaml</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Update="Views\AddGchDialog.axaml.cs">
      <DependentUpon>AddGchDialog.axaml</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <None Remove="devices.json" />
    <Content Include="Resources\Devices\**\*.xlsx">
      <CopyToOutputDirectory>Always</CopyToOutputDirectory>
    </Content>
    <Content Include="Resources\Devices\devices.json">
      <CopyToOutputDirectory>PreserveNewest</CopyToOutputDirectory>
    </Content>
    <Content Include="Resources\Devices\GCh_D2_List.json">
      <CopyToOutputDirectory>PreserveNewest</CopyToOutputDirectory>
    </Content>
  </ItemGroup>

  <Target Name="PostBuildCopyToTemplates" AfterTargets="Build">
    <PropertyGroup>
      <!-- Куда хост (ядро) смотрит на старте: AppContext.BaseDirectory/Templates -->
      <TemplatesDir>$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\..\DocumentTemplates\bin\$(Configuration)\$(TargetFramework)\Templates\</TemplatesDir>
    </PropertyGroup>
    <ItemGroup>
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)$(AssemblyName).dll" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)$(AssemblyName).deps.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(AssemblyName).deps.json')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)$(AssemblyName).runtimeconfig.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(AssemblyName).runtimeconfig.json')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)devices.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)devices.json')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)GCh_D2_List.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)GCh_D2_List.json')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)Resources\Devices\**\*.xlsx" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)Resources\Devices\devices.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Resources\Devices\devices.json')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)Resources\Devices\GCh_D2_List.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Resources\Devices\GCh_D2_List.json')" />
      <!-- Copy DocX dependencies into Templates folder so the plugin can resolve them when loaded via Assembly.LoadFrom -->
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)Xceed*.dll" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)SixLabors*.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)SixLabors*.dll')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)System.Drawing.Common.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)System.Drawing.Common.dll')" />
      <PluginFiles Include="$(TargetDir)System.IO.Packaging.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)System.IO.Packaging.dll')" />
    </ItemGroup>
    <MakeDir Directories="$(TemplatesDir)" />
    <!-- Всегда перезаписываем, чтобы в Templates лежала свежая сборка -->
    <Copy SourceFiles="@(PluginFiles)" DestinationFiles="@(PluginFiles -> '$(TemplatesDir)%(RecursiveDir)%(Filename)%(Extension)')" />
  </Target>

</Project>
